		<P CLASS="NL_m" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:48pt;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:-24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">4.<FONT STYLE="width: 20px;display: inline-block;">&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;</FONT>To approve our entering into an indemnification and exculpation agreement with Mr.&#x00a0;Uri Botzer, subject to his election as a director under Proposal&#x00a0;1.</P>

		<P CLASS="NL_m" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:48pt;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:-24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">6.<FONT STYLE="width: 20px;display: inline-block;">&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;</FONT>To approve the adoption of the U.S.&#x00a0;Taxpayers Appendix to the Kamada Ltd. 2011 Israeli Share Award Plan, for U.S.&#x00a0;tax purposes.</P>
		<P CLASS="NL_m" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:48pt;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:-24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">7.<FONT STYLE="width: 20px;display: inline-block;">&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;</FONT>To ratify and approve the re<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-appointment</FONT> of Kost Forer Gabbay&#x00a0;&amp; Kasierer, a member of Ernst&#x00a0;&amp; Young Global, as our independent registered public accountants for the year ending December&#x00a0;31, 2022 and for such additional period until our next annual general meeting of shareholders.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">In addition, at the Meeting, representatives of our management will be available to review and discuss our consolidated financial statements for the year ended December&#x00a0;31, 2021.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Our Board of Directors recommends that you vote &#x201c;FOR&#x201d; the election of each of the director nominees set forth in Proposal&#x00a0;1 and each of the other proposals that are described in the attached Proxy Statement.</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Shareholders of record at the close of business on November&#x00a0;14, 2022, are entitled to notice of and to vote at the Meeting. You can vote either by mailing in your proxy or in person by attending the Meeting. If voting by proxy, we will generally not be able to include your vote in the tally of ordinary shares voted at the Meeting unless your proxy is received by our transfer agent or at our registered office in Israel at least 48&#x00a0;hours prior to the appointed time of the Meeting. If you attend the Meeting, you may revoke your proxy (if previously submitted) and vote in person. If you are a beneficial owner of shares registered in the name of your broker, bank, trustee or nominee and you wish to vote in person at the Meeting, you must first obtain a &#x201c;legal proxy&#x201d; from your broker, bank, trustee or nominee that holds your shares giving you the right to vote the shares at the Meeting. Detailed proxy voting instructions are provided both in the Proxy Statement and on the enclosed proxy card.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">If you are a beneficial owner of shares registered in the name of a member of the Tel Aviv Stock Exchange (&#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">TASE</FONT>&#x201d;) and wish to vote, either by proxy or in person by attending the Meeting, you must deliver to us a proof of ownership in accordance with the Israeli Companies Law, 1999 (the &#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Israeli Companies Law</FONT>&#x201d;) and the Israeli Companies Regulations (Proof of Ownership of Shares for Voting at General Meetings), 2000. Such certification may be obtained at the TASE Member&#x2019;s offices or may be sent to the shareholder by mail (subject to payment of the cost of mailing), at the election of the shareholder, provided that the shareholder&#x2019;s request is submitted with respect to a specific securities account. Shareholders who hold shares through members of the TASE may also vote electronically </P>
		</DIV>

		<P CLASS="RRH" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:0;orphans:1;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin: 10px 0px 10px 0pt; page-break-after: always; border-bottom: 1px solid; margin-top: 10.0pt; margin-right: 0in; margin-bottom: 10.0pt; margin-left: 0in; text-align: center; font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: TimesNewRomanPSStd-Regular; color: black;">&#x00a0;</P>


	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;text-indent:0pt;margin-top:8pt;">via the electronic voting system of the Israel Securities Authority (&#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">ISA</FONT>&#x201d;) up to six&#x00a0;hours before the time fixed for the Meeting. You should receive instructions about electronic voting from the TASE member through which you hold your shares.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Shareholders may review the full version of the proposed resolutions in the Proxy Statement as well as the accompanying proxy card, via the website of the U.S.&#x00a0;Securities and Exchange Commission at <FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">www.sec.gov </FONT>or via the ISA&#x2019;s electronic filing system at <FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">http:</FONT><FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">//</FONT><FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">www.magna.isa.gov.il</FONT> or the website of the TASE at <FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">http:</FONT><FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">//</FONT><FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">maya.tase.co.il</FONT>, and also at our offices upon prior notice and during regular business&#x00a0;hours (2 Holzman Street, Weizmann Science Park, Rehovot, Israel; Tel: +972<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-8-9406472</FONT> (phone)) until the date of the Meeting. Our Company&#x2019;s representative is Ms. Yifat Philip, our Vice President General Counsel and Corporate Secretary (2 Holzman Street, Weizmann Science Park, Rehovot, Israel; Tel: +972<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-54-4710092</FONT>).</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Quorum</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The presence (in person, by proxy or via the ISA&#x2019;s electronic voting system) of two or more shareholders holding or representing, in the aggregate, at least twenty<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-five</FONT> percent of our company&#x2019;s voting rights will constitute a quorum at the Meeting. No business will be considered or determined at the Meeting unless the requisite quorum is present within half an hour from the time designated for the Meeting. If within half an hour from the time designated for the Meeting a quorum is not present, the Meeting will stand adjourned to the same&#x00a0;day in the following week, at the same time and place. Any number of shareholders present (in person or by proxy or via the ISA&#x2019;s electronic voting system) will constitute a quorum at the adjourned meeting. This notice will serve as notice of such reconvened meeting if no quorum is present at the original date and time and no further notice of the reconvened meeting will be given to shareholders.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Vote Required for Approval of the Proposals</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Each ordinary share entitles the holder to one vote.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The affirmative vote of the holders of a majority of the ordinary shares represented at the Meeting, in person, by proxy or via the ISA&#x2019;s electronic voting system, entitled to vote and voting on the matter, is required to elect each of the director nominees set forth in Proposal&#x00a0;1 and to approve each of the other proposals.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">In addition to the foregoing majority requirement, the approval of each of Proposal&#x00a0;3 and Proposal&#x00a0;5 is also subject to the fulfillment of one of the following additional voting requirements: (i)&#x00a0;the shares voting in favor of the proposal (excluding abstentions) include at least a majority of the shares voted by shareholders who are not controlling shareholders and shareholders who do not have a personal interest in the proposal, or (ii)&#x00a0;the total number of shares voted against the proposal by shareholders who are not controlling shareholders and shareholders who do not have a personal interest in the proposal does not exceed two<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-percent</FONT> (2%) of our outstanding voting rights.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">We are unaware of any shareholder that would be deemed to be a controlling shareholder of our company as of the current time for purposes of Proposals 3 and 5. <FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">A shareholder who signs and returns a proxy card will be deemed to be confirming that such shareholder, and any related party of such shareholder, is not a controlling shareholder for purposes of Proposals 3 and 5. If you believe that you, or a related party of yours, may be deemed to be a controlling shareholder and you wish to participate in the vote on any of Proposals 3 or</FONT><FONT CLASS="nobreak"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;"> </FONT></FONT><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">5, you should contact Ms. Yifat Philip, our Vice President General Counsel and Corporate Secretary (Tel: +972</FONT><FONT CLASS="nobreak"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">-54-4710092</FONT></FONT><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">, yifatp@kamada.com).</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">The Israeli Companies Law requires that each shareholder voting on Proposals 3 and 5 indicate on the proxy card or via the ISA&#x2019;s electronic voting system, or, if voting in person at the Meeting, inform us prior to voting on the matter at the Meeting, whether or not the shareholder has a personal interest in each such proposal.<FONT STYLE="width: 13px;display: inline-block;">&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;</FONT>Otherwise, the shareholder is not eligible to vote on the proposals and his or her vote will not be counted for the purposes of the proposals. </FONT>Under the Israeli Companies Law, a &#x201c;personal interest&#x201d; of a shareholder in an act or transaction of a company (i)&#x00a0;includes a personal interest of (a)&#x00a0;any spouse, sibling, parent, grandparent or descendant of the shareholder, any descendant, sibling or parent of a spouse of the shareholder and the spouse of any of the foregoing; and (b)&#x00a0;a company with respect to which the shareholder (or any of the foregoing relatives of the </P>
		</DIV>

		<P CLASS="LRH" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:0;orphans:1;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin: 10px 0px 10px 0pt; page-break-after: always; border-bottom: 1px solid; margin-top: 10.0pt; margin-right: 0in; margin-bottom: 10.0pt; margin-left: 0in; text-align: center; font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: TimesNewRomanPSStd-Regular; color: black;">&#x00a0;</P>


	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;text-indent:0pt;margin-top:8pt;">shareholder) serves as a director or chief executive officer, owns at least 5% of the outstanding shares or voting rights or has the right to appoint one or more directors or the chief executive officer; and (ii)&#x00a0;excludes a personal interest arising solely from the ownership of shares. Under the Israeli Companies Law, in the case of a person voting by proxy, &#x201c;personal interest&#x201d; includes the personal interest of either the proxy holder or the shareholder granting the proxy, whether or not the proxy holder has discretion how to vote.</P>

		<P CLASS="RRH" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:0;orphans:1;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin: 10px 0px 10px 0pt; page-break-after: always; border-bottom: 1px solid; margin-top: 10.0pt; margin-right: 0in; margin-bottom: 10.0pt; margin-left: 0in; text-align: center; font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: TimesNewRomanPSStd-Regular; color: black;">&#x00a0;</P>


	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">
		<P CLASS="H1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:1;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">KAMADA LTD.<BR></FONT><FONT CLASS="CharOverride-1" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;">2 Holzman Street<BR>Weizmann Science Park<BR>P.O.&#x00a0;Box 4081<BR>Rehovot 7670402, Israel</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="H1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:1;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">PROXY STATEMENT</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="H1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:1;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">ANNUAL GENERAL MEETING OF SHAREHOLDERS</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">This Proxy Statement is being furnished in connection with the solicitation of proxies on behalf of the Board of Directors of Kamada Ltd. (&#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">we</FONT>,&#x201d; &#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">us</FONT>,&#x201d; &#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">our</FONT>,&#x201d; or the &#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Company</FONT>&#x201d;) to be voted at the Annual General Meeting of Shareholders (the &#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Meeting</FONT>&#x201d;), and at any adjournment thereof, pursuant to the accompanying Notice of Annual General Meeting of Shareholders. The Meeting will be held at our offices at 2 Holzman Street, Weizmann Science Park, Rehovot, Israel, on Thursday, December&#x00a0;22, 2022, at 2:00&#x00a0;p.m. (Israel time).</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Purpose of the Annual General Meeting</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">At the Meeting, shareholders will be asked to consider and vote upon the following: (1)&#x00a0;election of nine directors to serve as members of our Board of Directors until our next annual general meeting of shareholders and until their successors are duly elected by the shareholders of the Company; (2)&#x00a0;approval of the grant of options to each of the director nominees, subject to their respective election as directors under Proposal&#x00a0;1; (3)&#x00a0;approval of the grant of options to Mr.&#x00a0;Amir London, our Chief Executive Officer; (4)&#x00a0;approval of our entering into an indemnification and exculpation agreement with Mr.&#x00a0;Uri Botzer, subject to his election as a director under Proposal&#x00a0;1; (5)&#x00a0;approval of the amendment and readoption of the Company&#x2019;s Compensation Policy for Executive Officers and Compensation Policy for Directors; (6)&#x00a0;approval of the adoption of the U.S.&#x00a0;Taxpayers Appendix to the Kamada Ltd. 2011 Israeli Share Award Plan, for U.S.&#x00a0;tax purposes; and (7)&#x00a0;ratification and approval of the re<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-appointment</FONT> of Kost Forer Gabbay&#x00a0;&amp; Kasierer, a member of Ernst&#x00a0;&amp; Young Global, as our independent registered public accountants for the year ending December&#x00a0;31, 2022 and for such additional period until our next annual general meeting of shareholders. In addition, at the Meeting, representatives of our management will be available to review and discuss our consolidated financial statements for the year ended December&#x00a0;31, 2021.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">We are not aware of any other matters that will come before the Meeting. If any other matters properly come before the Meeting, the persons designated as proxies intend to vote on such matters in accordance with the judgment and recommendation of the Board of Directors.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Recommendation of the Board of Directors</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Our Board of Directors recommends a vote FOR the election of each of the director nominees set forth in Proposal&#x00a0;1 and each of the other proposals set forth in this Proxy Statement.</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Who Can Vote</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">You are entitled to notice of, and to vote in person or by proxy at, the Meeting, if you are a holder of record of our ordinary shares as of the close of business on November&#x00a0;14, 2022. You are also entitled to notice of the Meeting and to vote at the Meeting if you held ordinary shares through a bank, broker or other nominee that is one of our shareholders of record at the close of business on November&#x00a0;14, 2022, or which appeared in the participant listing of a securities depository on that date. See below &#x201c;How You Can Vote.&#x201d;</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">How You Can Vote</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="BL_m" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;list-style-position:outside;list-style-type:disc;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:48pt;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:-24pt;widows:3;list-style-type:none;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="bullet" STYLE="font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">&#x2022;<FONT STYLE="width: 27px;display: inline-block;">&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;</FONT></FONT><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Voting in Person.&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;</FONT>If your shares are registered directly in your name with our transfer agent, American Stock Transfer&#x00a0;&amp; Trust Company LLC, or in our register of shareholders (i.e., you are a &#x201c;registered shareholder&#x201d;), you may attend and vote in person at the Meeting. If you are a beneficial owner of shares registered in the name of your broker, bank, trustee or nominee (i.e., your shares are held in &#x201c;street name&#x201d;), you are also invited to attend the Meeting; however, to vote in person at the Meeting as a beneficial owner, you must first obtain a &#x201c;legal proxy&#x201d; from your broker, bank, trustee or nominee that holds your shares giving you the right to vote the shares at the Meeting.</P>
		</DIV>

		<P CLASS="RRH" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:0;orphans:1;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin: 10px 0px 10px 0pt; page-break-after: always; border-bottom: 1px solid; margin-top: 10.0pt; margin-right: 0in; margin-bottom: 10.0pt; margin-left: 0in; text-align: center; font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: TimesNewRomanPSStd-Regular; color: black;">1</P>



	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">
		<P CLASS="BL_m" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;list-style-position:outside;list-style-type:disc;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:48pt;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:-24pt;widows:3;list-style-type:none;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="bullet" STYLE="font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">&#x2022;<FONT STYLE="width: 27px;display: inline-block;">&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;</FONT></FONT><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Voting by Proxy.&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;</FONT>You may submit your proxy by mail by completing, signing and mailing the enclosed proxy card in the enclosed, postage<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-paid</FONT> envelope, or, if your shares are held in &#x201c;street name,&#x201d; by following the voting instructions provided by your broker, bank trustee or nominee. We will generally not be able to include your vote in the tally of ordinary shares voted at the Meeting unless your proxy is received by our transfer agent or at our registered office in Israel at least 48&#x00a0;hours prior to the designated time for the Meeting. Upon receipt of a properly signed and dated proxy in the form enclosed, the persons named as proxies therein will vote the ordinary shares represented thereby in accordance with the instructions of the shareholder indicated thereon, or if directions are not given or directions are not in accordance with the options listed on a proxy card, such shares will be voted in accordance with the recommendations of our Board of Directors.</P>
		<P CLASS="BL_m" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;list-style-position:outside;list-style-type:disc;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:48pt;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:-24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="bullet" STYLE="font-size:10pt;">&#x2022;<FONT STYLE="width: 27px;display: inline-block;">&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;</FONT></FONT><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Shares Traded on TASE.&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;</FONT>Shareholders who hold shares through members of the Tel Aviv Stock Exchange (the &#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">TASE</FONT>&#x201d;) may vote in person or through the enclosed form of proxy by completing, signing, dating and mailing the proxy with a copy of their identity card, passport or certificate of incorporation, as the case may be, to the Company&#x2019;s office in Israel. Shareholders who hold shares through members of the TASE and intend to vote their shares either in person or by proxy, must deliver to the Company an ownership certificate confirming their ownership of the Company&#x2019;s shares on the record date (&#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Ownership Certificate</FONT>&#x201d;), which must be certified by a recognized financial institution, as required by the Israeli Companies Regulations (Proof of Ownership of Shares for Voting at General Meeting) of 2000. Generally, a proxy (together with the Ownership Certificate) must be received at our registered office in Israel at least 48&#x00a0;hours prior to the designated time for the Meeting to be included in the tally of ordinary shares voted at the Meeting. Alternatively, shareholders who hold shares through members of the TASE may vote electronically via the electronic voting system of the Israel Securities Authority (&#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">ISA</FONT>&#x201d;) up to six&#x00a0;hours before the time fixed for the Meeting. You should receive instructions about electronic voting from the TASE member through which you hold your shares.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Change or Revocation of Proxy</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">If you are a shareholder of record, you may change your vote at any time prior to the exercise of authority granted in the proxy by delivering a written notice of revocation to our General Counsel and Corporate Secretary, by granting a new proxy bearing a later date, or by attending the Meeting and voting in person. Attendance at the Meeting will not cause your previously granted proxy to be revoked unless you specifically so request.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">If your shares are held in &#x201c;street name,&#x201d; you may change your vote by submitting new voting instructions to your broker, bank, trustee or nominee or, if you have obtained a legal proxy from your broker, bank, trustee or nominee giving you the right to vote your shares, by attending the Meeting and voting in person.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">If you are a beneficial owner of shares registered in the name of a member of the TASE, you may change your vote (i)&#x00a0;by attending the Meeting and voting in person, by presenting a valid Ownership Certificate as of the record date; (ii)&#x00a0;by delivering a later<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-dated</FONT> duly executed proxy, together with a valid Ownership Certificate as of the record date, to the Company&#x2019;s registered office in Israel at least 48&#x00a0;hours prior to the designated time for the Meeting, or (iii)&#x00a0;by following the relevant instructions for changing your vote via the ISA&#x2019;s electronic voting system by no later than six&#x00a0;hours before the time set for the Meeting.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Quorum</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The presence (in person, by proxy or via the ISA&#x2019;s electronic voting system), of two or more shareholders holding or representing, in the aggregate, at least twenty<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-five</FONT> percent of the Company&#x2019;s voting rights will constitute a quorum at the Meeting. No business will be considered or determined at the Meeting unless the requisite quorum is present within half an hour from the time designated for the Meeting. If within half an hour from the time designated for the Meeting a quorum is not present, the Meeting will stand adjourned to the same&#x00a0;day in the following week, at the same time and place. Any number of shareholders present (in person, by proxy or via the ISA&#x2019;s electronic voting system), will constitute a quorum at the adjourned meeting. This notice will serve as notice of such reconvened meeting if no quorum is present at the original date and time and no further notice of the reconvened meeting will be given to shareholders.</P>
		</DIV>

		<P CLASS="LRH" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:0;orphans:1;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin: 10px 0px 10px 0pt; page-break-after: always; border-bottom: 1px solid; margin-top: 10.0pt; margin-right: 0in; margin-bottom: 10.0pt; margin-left: 0in; text-align: center; font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: TimesNewRomanPSStd-Regular; color: black;">2</P>



	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Abstentions and broker non<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-votes</FONT> will be counted towards the quorum. Broker non<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-votes</FONT> occur when brokers that hold their customers&#x2019; shares in street name sign and submit proxies for such shares, and vote such shares on some matters but not on others. This occurs when brokers have not received any instructions from their customers, in which case the brokers, as the holders of record, are permitted to vote on &#x201c;routine&#x201d; matters, but not on non<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-routine</FONT> matters.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Unsigned or unreturned proxies, including those not returned by banks, brokers, or other record holders, will not be counted for quorum or voting purposes.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Vote Required for Approval of the Proposals</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Each ordinary share entitles the holder to one vote.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The affirmative vote of the holders of a majority of the ordinary shares represented at the Meeting, in person, by proxy or via the ISA&#x2019;s electronic voting system, entitled to vote and voting on the matter, is required to elect each of the director nominees set forth in Proposal&#x00a0;1 and to approve each of the other proposals.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">In addition to the foregoing majority requirement, the approval of each of Proposal&#x00a0;3 and Proposal&#x00a0;5 is also subject to the fulfillment of one of the following additional voting requirements (the &#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Special Majority</FONT>&#x201d;): (i)&#x00a0;the shares voting in favor of the proposal (excluding abstentions) include at least a majority of the shares voted by shareholders who are not controlling shareholders and shareholders who do not have a personal interest in the proposal, or (ii)&#x00a0;the total number of shares voted against the proposal by shareholders who are not controlling shareholders and shareholders who do not have a personal interest in the proposal does not exceed two<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-percent</FONT> (2%) of our outstanding voting rights.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">We are unaware of any shareholder that would be deemed to be a controlling shareholder of our company as of the current time for purposes of Proposals 3 and 5. <FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">A shareholder who signs and returns a proxy card will be deemed to be confirming that such shareholder, and any related party of such shareholder, is not a controlling shareholder for purposes of Proposals 3 and 5. If you believe that you, or a related party of yours, may be deemed to be a controlling shareholder and you wish to participate in the vote on any of Proposals 3 and</FONT><FONT CLASS="nobreak"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;"> </FONT></FONT><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">5, you should contact Ms. Yifat Philip, our Vice President General Counsel and Corporate Secretary (Tel: +972</FONT><FONT CLASS="nobreak"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">-54-4710092</FONT></FONT><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">, yifatp@kamada.com).</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">The Israeli Companies Law requires that each shareholder voting on Proposals 3 and 5 indicate on the proxy card or via the ISA&#x2019;s electronic voting system, or, if voting in person at the Meeting, inform us prior to voting on the matter at the Meeting, whether or not the shareholder has a personal interest in each such proposal.<FONT STYLE="width: 13px;display: inline-block;">&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;</FONT>Otherwise, the shareholder is not eligible to vote on the proposals and his or her vote will not be counted for the purposes of the proposals. </FONT>Under the Israeli Companies Law, a &#x201c;personal interest&#x201d; of a shareholder in an act or transaction of a company (i)&#x00a0;includes a personal interest of (a)&#x00a0;any spouse, sibling, parent, grandparent or descendant of the shareholder, any descendant, sibling or parent of a spouse of the shareholder and the spouse of any of the foregoing; and (b)&#x00a0;a company with respect to which the shareholder (or any of the foregoing relatives of the shareholder) serves as a director or chief executive officer, owns at least 5% of the outstanding shares or voting rights or has the right to appoint one or more directors or the chief executive officer; and (ii)&#x00a0;excludes a personal interest arising solely from the ownership of shares. Under the Israeli Companies Law, in the case of a person voting by proxy, &#x201c;personal interest&#x201d; includes the personal interest of either the proxy holder or the shareholder granting the proxy, whether or not the proxy holder has discretion how to vote.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">In tabulating the voting results for any particular proposal, shares that constitute broker non<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-votes</FONT> and abstentions are not considered votes cast on that proposal. Unsigned or unreturned proxies, including those not returned by banks, brokers, or other record holders, will not be counted for voting purposes.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Position Statements</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">In accordance with the Israeli Companies Law and regulations promulgated thereunder, any shareholder may submit to us a position statement on its behalf, expressing its position on an agenda item for the Meeting to our offices, 2 Holzman Street, Weizmann Science Park, Rehovot, Israel, Attention: Ms. Yifat Philip, Vice President, General Counsel and Corporate Secretary, or by facsimile to +972<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-8-9406473</FONT>, no later than Monday, December&#x00a0;12, 2022 at 2:00&#x00a0;p.m. Israel time.</P>
		</DIV>

		<P CLASS="RRH" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:0;orphans:1;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin: 10px 0px 10px 0pt; page-break-after: always; border-bottom: 1px solid; margin-top: 10.0pt; margin-right: 0in; margin-bottom: 10.0pt; margin-left: 0in; text-align: center; font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: TimesNewRomanPSStd-Regular; color: black;">3</P>



	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Cost of Soliciting Votes for the Meeting</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">We will bear the cost of soliciting proxies from our shareholders. Proxies will be solicited by mail and may also be solicited in person, by telephone or electronic communication, by our directors, officers and employees. We will reimburse brokerage houses and other custodians, nominees and fiduciaries for their expenses in accordance with the regulations of the U.S.&#x00a0;Securities and Exchange Commission (the &#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">SEC</FONT>&#x201d;) concerning the sending of proxies and proxy material to the beneficial owners of our shares.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Security Ownership of Certain Beneficial Owners and Management</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The following table sets forth certain information as of November&#x00a0;1, 2022 (unless otherwise indicated below) regarding the beneficial ownership by (i)&#x00a0;each person known to us to beneficially own more than 5% of our outstanding ordinary shares; (ii)&#x00a0;each of our current directors and director nominees; and (iii)&#x00a0;all of our current directors and executive officers as a group.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The percentage of beneficial ownership of our ordinary shares is based on 44,826,460 ordinary shares outstanding as of November&#x00a0;1, 2022. Beneficial ownership is determined in accordance with the rules of the SEC and generally includes voting power or investment power with respect to securities. All options exercisable into ordinary shares within 60&#x00a0;days of the date of the table are deemed to be outstanding and beneficially owned by the person holding such options for the purpose of computing the number of shares beneficially owned by such shareholder. Such shares are also deemed outstanding for purposes of computing the percentage ownership of the person holding the options. They are not, however, deemed to be outstanding and beneficially owned for the purpose of computing the percentage ownership of any other person or entity.</P>

	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">
		<P CLASS="H1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:1;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">PROPOSAL&#x00a0;1<BR>ELECTION OF DIRECTORS<BR></FONT><FONT CLASS="CharOverride-1" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;">(Item&#x00a0;1 on the Proxy Card)</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Background</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Under our articles of association, the number of directors on our Board of Directors shall be no less than five and no more than 11. Our Board of Directors is currently comprised of nine directors, all of whom were elected to serve in such capacity at our 2021 annual general meeting of shareholders. Each of our directors generally holds office until the first annual general meeting of shareholders following his or her appointment (unless the tenure of such director expires earlier, or a director is removed from office pursuant to the Israeli Companies Law).</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Each of our currently serving directors, other than Mr.&#x00a0;Amiram Boehm, is standing for re<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-election</FONT> at the Meeting, to hold office until our next annual general meeting of shareholders and until his or her successor is elected and qualified, subject to our articles of association and applicable law. In addition, Mr.&#x00a0;Uri Botzer shall be standing for election as a director for the first time at the Meeting, to hold office until our next annual general meeting of shareholders and until his successor is elected and qualified, subject to our articles of association and applicable law. Our Board of Directors has affirmatively determined that each of Mrs.&#x00a0;Lilach Asher Topilsky, Mr.&#x00a0;Uri Botzer, Mr.&#x00a0;Ishay Davidi, Ms. Karnit Goldwasser, Ms. Lilach Payorski, Mr.&#x00a0;Leon Recanati, Mr.&#x00a0;David Tsur and Prof. Ari Shamiss is an &#x201c;independent director&#x201d; as defined under NASDAQ Listing Rules. Accordingly, subject to shareholder approval of the above director nominees, our Board of Directors will consist of nine members, eight of whom satisfy the independence requirements of the NASDAQ Listing Rules.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">In accordance with the Israeli Companies Law, each of the director nominees has certified to us that he or she meets all the requirements of the Israeli Companies Law for election as a director of a public company, possesses the necessary qualifications and is able to dedicate sufficient time to fulfill his or her duties as a director of the Company, taking into consideration our company&#x2019;s size and special needs.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Nominees for Director</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The following biographical information is provided with respect to each director nominee based upon our records and information provided to us by each nominee.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="BoldItalic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:bold;">Mrs. Lilach Asher Topilsky</FONT> has served as a member of our board of directors since December&#x00a0;2019, as the Chairman of our board of directors since August&#x00a0;2020, and serves as a member of our Compensation Committee and Strategy Committee. Mrs.&#x00a0;Asher Topilsky has been a Senior Partner in the FIMI Opportunity Funds, Israel&#x2019;s largest group of private equity funds, since December&#x00a0;2019. Mrs.&#x00a0;Asher Topilsky currently serves as the chairman of G1 Security Systems Ltd. (TASE), Rimoni Industries Ltd. (TASE), SOS Ltd. Elyakim Ben Ari Group Ltd. and Amal and beyond Ltd. and as a director at Amiad Water Systems Ltd. (AIM), Ashot Ashkelon Industries Ltd. (TASE) and Tel Aviv University. Prior to joining FIMI, Mrs.&#x00a0;Asher Topilsky served as the President and CEO of Israel Discount Bank (TASE), one of the leading banking groups in Israel, as the Chairman at IDBNY BANKCORP and as a director at IDB Bank New&#x00a0;York from 2014 <FONT CLASS="nobreak">-2019</FONT>. Mrs.&#x00a0;Asher Topilsky also served as the Chairman of Mercantile Bank from&#x00a0;2014<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-2016</FONT>. Before that, Mrs.&#x00a0;Asher Topilsky served as a member of the management of Bank Hapoalim (TASE) as Deputy CEO&#x00a0;&amp; Head of Retail Banking Division (2009<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-2013</FONT>)&#x00a0;&amp; Head of Strategy&#x00a0;&amp; Planning Division (2007<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-2009</FONT>). Mrs.&#x00a0;Asher Topilsky served as a Strategy Consultant at The Boston Consulting Group (BCG, Chicago&#x00a0;1997<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-1998</FONT>) and at Shaldor Strategy Consulting (Israel&#x00a0;1995<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-1996</FONT>). Mrs.&#x00a0;Asher Topilsky holds an M.B.A. degree from Kellogg School of Management, Northwestern University, Chicago, USA (1997), and a B.A. degree in Management and Economics from Tel Aviv University, Israel (Magna Cum Laude, 1994).</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="BoldItalic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:bold;">Mr.&#x00a0;Uri Botzer</FONT> has been a Junior Partner in the FIMI Opportunity Funds, Israel&#x2019;s largest group of private equity funds, since 2019. In accordance with Israeli law and practice, directors are recommended by our Board of Directors for election by our shareholders. Our Board of Directors reviewed the qualifications and suitability of each of the director nominees and our Board of Directors as a whole. We believe that our Board of Directors contains highly qualified and talented directors, including directors with global biopharmaceutical and financial experience. Accordingly, our Board of Directors approved the nomination of each of the director nominees.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Under a voting agreement entered into on March&#x00a0;6, 2013, the Recanati Group, on the one hand, and the Damar Group, on the other hand, each agreed to vote the ordinary shares beneficially owned by them in favor of the election of director nominees designated by the other group as follows: (i)&#x00a0;three director nominees, so long as the other group beneficially owns at least 7.5% of our outstanding share capital, (ii)&#x00a0;two director nominees, so long as the other group beneficially owns at least 5.0% (but less than 7.5%) of our outstanding share capital, and (iii)&#x00a0;one director nominee, so long as the other group beneficially owns at least 2.5% (but less than 5.0%) of our outstanding share capital. In addition, to the extent that after the designation of the foregoing director nominees there are additional director vacancies, each of the Recanati Group and Damar Group have agreed to vote the ordinary shares beneficially owned by them in favor of such additional director nominees designated by the party who beneficially owns the larger voting rights in the Company.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">We are not aware of any reason why the nominees, if elected, would be unable or unwilling to serve as directors. 		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">The Board of Directors recommends a vote FOR the election of each nominee for director named above.</FONT></P>

		<P CLASS="LRH" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:0;orphans:1;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin: 10px 0px 10px 0pt; page-break-after: always; border-bottom: 1px solid; margin-top: 10.0pt; margin-right: 0in; margin-bottom: 10.0pt; margin-left: 0in; text-align: center; font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: TimesNewRomanPSStd-Regular; color: black;">10</P>



	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">
		<P CLASS="H1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:1;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">PROPOSAL&#x00a0;2<BR>APPROVAL OF THE GRANT OF OPTIONS TO EACH OF THE DIRECTOR NOMINEES<BR></FONT><FONT CLASS="CharOverride-1" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;">(Item&#x00a0;2 on the Proxy Card)</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Background</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Under the Israeli Companies Law, the payment of compensation, including the grant of options, to a director, which is consistent with a company&#x2019;s compensation policy must be approved by the compensation committee, board of directors and shareholders (in that order).</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">During 2022, our Compensation Committee and Board of Directors conducted a comprehensive periodic review of our equity<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-based</FONT> compensation awards to our management members and directors. Based on such review, our Compensation Committee and Board of Directors determined that, as an incentive for the activities and efforts of our management and directors, it would be appropriate and in the Company&#x2019;s best interest to award to our management members and directors equity<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-based</FONT> compensation, in the form of options, once every three&#x00a0;years, according to a pre<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-determined</FONT> three<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-year</FONT> share<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-based</FONT> compensation award plan, in lieu of awarding equity<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-based</FONT> compensation awards on an annual basis. Accordingly, our Compensation Committee and Board of Directors adopted a three<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-year</FONT> share<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-based</FONT> compensation award plan and, in accordance with such plan, approved, subject to shareholder approval, the grant to each of the director nominees (other than Mr.&#x00a0;Uri Botzer), subject to their re<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-election</FONT> at the Meeting, of options to purchase 30,000 ordinary shares. The options shall be exercisable on a cashless basis based on an exercise price of NIS 19.36 (which exercise price is equal to the higher of (a)&#x00a0;the average closing price of our ordinary shares on the TASE during the 30&#x00a0;trading days prior to the date of the approval of the option grant by our Board of Directors and (b)&#x00a0;the closing price of our ordinary shares on the TASE on the date of the approval of the option grant by our Board of Directors, plus 5%).</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">In addition, in connection with Mr.&#x00a0;Uri Botzer&#x2019;s nomination to serve as a director, our Compensation Committee and Board of Directors approved, subject to shareholder approval, the grant to Mr.&#x00a0;Botzer, subject to his election at the Meeting, of options to purchase 30,000 ordinary shares. The options shall be exercisable on a cashless basis based on an exercise price of NIS 17.35 (which exercise price is equal to the higher of (a)&#x00a0;the average closing price of our ordinary shares on the TASE during the 30&#x00a0;trading days prior to the date of the approval of the option grant by our Board of Directors and (b)&#x00a0;the closing price of our ordinary shares on the TASE on the date of the approval of the option grant by our Board of Directors, plus 5%).</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The options, if approved at the Meeting, will be granted under our 2011 Israeli Share Award Plan (the &#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">2011 Plan</FONT>&#x201d;) and will vest over a period of four&#x00a0;years in four equal installments, such that 25% of the options will vest on each anniversary of the grant date. The options will be exercisable for 6.5&#x00a0;years following the date of grant and all unexercised options will expire immediately thereafter. The award of the options to each director nominee is subject to such director nominee&#x2019;s respective election at the Meeting under Proposal&#x00a0;1 (such that if any such director nominee is not elected at the Meeting, he or she shall not be entitled to the options).</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Each of our Compensation Committee and Board of Directors determined that the grant of the options to the director nominees and their terms are consistent with our Compensation Policy for Directors, as currently in effect and as proposed to be amended and readopted at the Meeting (see Proposal&#x00a0;5).</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Proposed Resolution</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">It is proposed that at the Meeting the following resolution be adopted:</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-left:24pt;text-indent:0pt;margin-top:8pt;">&#x201c;RESOLVED, to approve the grant to each of the director nominees of options to purchase 30,000 ordinary shares of the Company, with such terms and conditions as set forth in the Proxy Statement for the Meeting, subject to their respective election as a director at the Meeting.&#x201d;</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Approval Required</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-left:24pt;text-indent:0pt;margin-top:8pt;">See &#x201c;Vote Required for Approval of the Proposals&#x201d; above.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Board Recommendation</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">The Board of Directors recommends a vote FOR the approval of the grant of options to each of the director nominees, subject to their respective election at the Meeting.</FONT></P>
