		<P CLASS="RRH" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:0;orphans:1;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin: 10px 0px 10px 0pt; page-break-after: always; border-bottom: 1px solid; margin-top: 10.0pt; margin-right: 0in; margin-bottom: 10.0pt; margin-left: 0in; text-align: center; font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: TimesNewRomanPSStd-Regular; color: black;">17</P>



	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">
		<P CLASS="H1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:1;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">PROPOSAL&#x00a0;6<BR>APPROVAL OF THE U.S.&#x00a0;TAXPAYERS APPENDIX TO THE 2011 ISRAELI SHARE AWARD PLAN, <BR>FOR U.S.&#x00a0;TAX PURPOSES<BR></FONT><FONT CLASS="CharOverride-1" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;">(Item&#x00a0;6 on the Proxy Card)</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Background</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">In July&#x00a0;2011, we adopted our 2011 Israeli Share Option Plan and in September&#x00a0;2016, we amended and renamed it as the 2011 Israeli Share Award Plan (the &#x201c;2011 Plan&#x201d;). The 2011 Plan expired in July&#x00a0;2021 and in August&#x00a0;2021, we extended the 2011 Plan by an additional ten&#x00a0;years, until August&#x00a0;9, 2031, and adopted a few additional amendments to the 2011 Plan. References below to the &#x201c;2011 Plan&#x201d; refer to the 2011 Plan as amended in August&#x00a0;2021.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Under the 2011 Plan, we are authorized to grant options and restricted shares to directors, officers, employees, consultants and service providers of our company and subsidiaries. The 2011 Plan is intended to enhance our ability to attract and retain desirable individuals by increasing their ownership interests in us. The 2011 Plan is designed to reflect the provisions of the Israeli Tax Ordinance [New Version], 1961, which affords certain tax advantages to Israeli employees, officers and directors. The 2011 Plan may be administered by our Board of Directors either directly or upon the recommendation of the Compensation Committee. For additional details regarding the 2011 Plan, see &#x201c;Item&#x00a0;6. Directors, Senior Management and Employees&#x00a0;&#x2014;&#x00a0;Equity Compensation Plans&#x201d; of our annual report on Form&#x00a0;20<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-F</FONT> for the year ended December&#x00a0;31, 2021, filed with the SEC on March&#x00a0;15, 2022.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">In February&#x00a0;2022, our Board of Directors adopted the U.S.&#x00a0;Taxpayers Appendix to the 2011 Plan, attached hereto as <FONT CLASS="CharOverride-3" STYLE="text-decoration:underline;">Appendix B</FONT> (the &#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">US</FONT> <FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Appendix</FONT>&#x201d;), which provides for the grant of options and restricted shares to persons who are subject to U.S.&#x00a0;federal income tax. The US Appendix provides for the grant to U.S.&#x00a0;employees of options that qualify as incentive stock options (&#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">ISOs</FONT>&#x201d;) under the U.S.&#x00a0;Internal Revenue Code of 1986, as amended (the &#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Code</FONT>&#x201d;), which affords certain tax advantages to U.S.&#x00a0;employees. Our Board of Directors determined, following our recent acquisitions that resulted in our acquisition of U.S.<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-based</FONT> operations and our acquisition and hiring of U.S.&#x00a0;based<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-employees</FONT>, that the adoption of the US Appendix is necessary to enhance our ability to attract and retain desirable individuals for employment in the United&#x00a0;States.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Although the Nasdaq Listing Rules generally require shareholder approval of equity compensation plans and material amendments thereto, pursuant to certain exemptions for foreign private issuers, we follow Israeli law and practice, under which the adoption of equity<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-based</FONT> compensation plans and amendments thereto are generally required to approved by the Board of Directors (and the grant of certain awards under such plans are approved by shareholders to the extent such approval is required for the specific grants under the Israeli Companies Law). However, in accordance with the provisions of the Code, the grant of ISOs under the US Appendix is subject to the approval of the US Appendix by our shareholders within 12&#x00a0;months of its approval by our Board of Directors. If this proposal is not approved by our shareholders, then the 2011 Plan will continue to be in effect, and we may issue awards under the 2011 Plan, but we will be unable to grant options to our U.S.&#x00a0;employees that qualify as ISOs for U.S.&#x00a0;federal tax purposes.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Description of the US Appendix</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Set forth below is a summary of the US Appendix, but this summary is qualified in its entirety by reference to the full text of the US Appendix, a copy of which is attached to this proxy statement as <FONT CLASS="CharOverride-3" STYLE="text-decoration:underline;">Appendix B</FONT>. Please see <FONT CLASS="CharOverride-3" STYLE="text-decoration:underline;">Appendix B</FONT>&#x00a0;for more detailed information.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The provisions of the US Appendix apply only to persons who are subject to U.S.&#x00a0;federal income tax. The US Appendix provides for the grant of options and restricted shares.</P>
		<P CLASS="H3" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:italic;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="BoldItalic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:bold;">Options</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Options granted under the US Appendix may include ISOs intended to qualify under Section&#x00a0;422 of the Code as well as options that do not or are not intended to qualify as ISOs under the Code (&#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Non</FONT><FONT CLASS="nobreak"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">-Qualified</FONT></FONT><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;"> Stock Options</FONT>&#x201d;). All employees, officers and directors of the Company or any affiliate thereof are eligible to be granted options that are Non<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-Qualified</FONT> Stock Options under the US Appendix, and only employees, officers and directors of the Company or any affiliate thereof are eligible to be granted ISOs under the US Appendix.</P>
		</DIV>

		<P CLASS="LRH" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:0;orphans:1;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin: 10px 0px 10px 0pt; page-break-after: always; border-bottom: 1px solid; margin-top: 10.0pt; margin-right: 0in; margin-bottom: 10.0pt; margin-left: 0in; text-align: center; font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: TimesNewRomanPSStd-Regular; color: black;">18</P>



	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">
		<P CLASS="H4" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:italic;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:1;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">Incentive Stock Options</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The purchase price per share subject to an ISO shall be determined by the Board of Directors at the time of grant, provided that it shall not be less than 100% of the fair market value of the share at the time of grant and if an ISO is granted to a shareholder possessing more than 10% of the total combined voting power of all classes of shares of the Company, its subsidiaries or its parent (a &#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Ten Percent Shareholder</FONT>&#x201d;), the purchase price per share shall be no less than 110% of the fair market value of the share at the time of the grant of such ISO.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The term of each ISO shall be fixed by the Board of Directors; provided, however, that no ISO shall be exercisable more than 10&#x00a0;years after the date of grant and if granted to a Ten Percent Shareholder, shall not exceed five&#x00a0;years.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">To the extent that the aggregate fair market value (determined as of the time of grant) of shares with respect to which ISOs are exercisable for the first time by an employee during any calendar year under the US Appendix and/or any other option plan of the Company, any subsidiary or any parent exceeds US$100,000, such ISOs shall be treated as Non<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-Qualified</FONT> Stock Options.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The aggregate maximum number of ordinary shares that may be issued upon the exercise of ISOs 500,000. Such maximum number of shares that may be issued upon the exercise of ISOs shall not be increased without the approval of the shareholders of the Company as required pursuant to the Code, provided, however, such maximum number of ordinary shares may be subject to further adjustment in the event of specified changes to the capital structure of the Company.</P>
		<P CLASS="H3" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:italic;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="BoldItalic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:bold;">Restricted Shares</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">A grant of restricted shares may, but is not required to, have a purchase price which may be set at the discretion of the Board of Directors. If, during the restricted period, the participant&#x2019;s service terminates, any shares remaining subject to restrictions will be forfeited. The Board of Directors has the authority to cancel any or all outstanding restrictions prior to the end of the restricted period, including in connection with certain types of termination of service.</P>
		<P CLASS="H3" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:italic;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="BoldItalic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:bold;">Amendment of the US Appendix</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The US Appendix may be amended or terminated in accordance with the terms governing the amendment or termination of the 2011 Plan; provided, however, that without the approval of the shareholders of the Company entitled to vote in accordance with applicable law, no amendment may be made that would: (i)&#x00a0;increase the aggregate number of shares that may be issued upon the exercise of ISOs; (ii)&#x00a0;change the classification of individuals eligible to receive ISOs under the US Appendix; (iii)&#x00a0;extend the term of the 2011 Plan; or (iv)&#x00a0;require shareholder approval in order to continue to comply with Section&#x00a0;422 of the Code to the extent applicable to ISOs.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">U.S Federal Income Tax Consequences</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The following is a brief summary of certain U.S.&#x00a0;federal income tax consequences relating to the transactions described under the US Appendix as set forth below. This summary does not purport to address all aspects of U.S.&#x00a0;federal income taxation and does not describe state, local, or foreign tax consequences. This discussion is based upon provisions of the Code and the Treasury Regulations issued thereunder, and judicial and administrative interpretations under the Code and the Treasury Regulations, all as in effect as of the date hereof, and all of which are subject to change (possibly on a retroactive basis) or different interpretation.</P>
		<P CLASS="H3" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:italic;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="BoldItalic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:bold;">Options</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">Incentive Stock Options.<FONT STYLE="width: 13px;display: inline-block;">&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;</FONT></FONT>A participant will not recognize income at the time an ISO is granted. When a participant exercises an ISO, a participant also generally will not be required to recognize income (either as ordinary income or capital gain), provided that the participant is employed by the Company or a qualifying subsidiary from the date of grant of the option until three&#x00a0;months prior to the exercise thereof, except where such employment or service terminates by reason of disability or death (where the three month period is extended to one year). To the extent the aforesaid employment requirement is not met at the time of exercise, the options will no longer qualify as ISOs and will be treated as Non<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-Qualified</FONT> Stock Options for federal tax purposes, as described below. Moreover, to the extent that the fair market value (determined as of the date of grant) of the shares with respect to which the participant&#x2019;s ISOs are exercisable for the first time during any year exceeds $100,000, the ISOs for the shares over $100,000 will </P>
		</DIV>

		<P CLASS="RRH" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:0;orphans:1;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin: 10px 0px 10px 0pt; page-break-after: always; border-bottom: 1px solid; margin-top: 10.0pt; margin-right: 0in; margin-bottom: 10.0pt; margin-left: 0in; text-align: center; font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: TimesNewRomanPSStd-Regular; color: black;">19</P>



	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;text-indent:0pt;margin-top:8pt;">be treated as Non<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-Qualified</FONT> Stock Options, and not ISOs, for federal tax purposes, and the participant will recognize income as if the ISOs were Non<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-Qualified</FONT> Stock Options. In addition to the foregoing, if the fair market value of the shares received upon exercise of an ISO exceeds the exercise price, then the excess may be deemed a tax preference adjustment for purposes of the federal alternative minimum tax calculation. The federal alternative minimum tax may produce significant tax repercussions depending upon the participant&#x2019;s particular tax status.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The tax treatment of any shares acquired by exercise of an ISO will depend upon whether the participant disposes of his or her shares prior to two&#x00a0;years after the date the ISO was granted or one year after the shares were transferred to the participant (referred to as, the &#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Holding Period</FONT>&#x201d;). If a participant disposes of shares acquired by exercise of an ISO after the expiration of the Holding Period, any amount received in excess of the participant&#x2019;s tax basis for such shares (<FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">e.g</FONT>., the exercise price) will be treated as capital gain. If the amount received is less than the participant&#x2019;s tax basis for such shares, the loss may be treated as short<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-term</FONT> or long<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-term</FONT> capital loss, depending upon how long the participant has held the shares.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">If the participant disposes of shares acquired by exercise of an ISO prior to the expiration of the Holding Period, the disposition will be considered a &#x201c;disqualifying disposition.&#x201d; If the amount received for the shares is greater than the fair market value of the shares on the exercise date, then the difference between the ISO&#x2019;s exercise price and the fair market value of the shares at the time of exercise will be treated as ordinary income for the tax year in which the &#x201c;disqualifying disposition&#x201d; occurs. The participant&#x2019;s basis in the shares will be increased by an amount equal to the amount treated as ordinary income due to such &#x201c;disqualifying disposition.&#x201d; In addition, the amount received in such &#x201c;disqualifying disposition&#x201d; over the participant&#x2019;s increased basis in the shares will be treated as short<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-term</FONT> or long<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-term</FONT> capital gain, depending how long the participant has held the shares. However, if the price received for shares acquired by exercise of an ISO is less than the fair market value of the shares on the exercise date and the disposition is a transaction in which the participant sustains a loss which otherwise would be recognizable under the Code, then the amount of ordinary income that the participant will recognize is the excess, if any, of the amount realized on the &#x201c;disqualifying disposition&#x201d; over the basis of the shares.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The Company, and/or the participant&#x2019;s employer as applicable, will not be allowed a federal income tax deduction upon the grant or exercise of an ISO or the disposition, after the applicable Holding Period, of the Company shares acquired upon exercise of an ISO.&#x00a0;In the event of a disqualifying disposition, the Company or the participant&#x2019;s employer, as applicable, generally will be entitled to a deduction in an amount equal to the ordinary income recognized by the participant, provided that such amount constitutes an ordinary and necessary business expense to the Company and is reasonable and the limitations of Code Sections 280G and 162(m)&#x00a0;do not apply.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">Non</FONT><FONT CLASS="nobreak"><FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">-Qualified</FONT></FONT><FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;"> Stock Options.&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;</FONT>A participant generally will not recognize income at the time a Non<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-Qualified</FONT> Stock Option is granted. When a participant exercises a Non<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-Qualified</FONT> Stock Option, the difference between the option price and any higher market value of the shares of common stock on the date of exercise will be treated as compensation taxable as ordinary income to the participant. The participant&#x2019;s tax basis for the shares acquired under a Non<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-Qualified</FONT> Stock Option will be equal to the option price paid for such shares, plus any amounts included in the participant&#x2019;s income as compensation, subject to income tax withholdings. When a participant disposes of shares acquired by exercise of a Non<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-Qualified</FONT> Stock Option, any amount received in excess of the participant&#x2019;s tax basis for such shares will be treated as short<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-term</FONT> or long<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-term</FONT> capital gain, depending upon how long the participant has held the shares. If the amount received is less than the participant&#x2019;s tax basis for such shares, the loss will be treated as short<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-term</FONT> or long<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-term</FONT> capital loss, depending upon how long the participant has held the shares.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">A federal income tax deduction generally will be allowed to a participant&#x2019;s employer in an amount equal to the ordinary income recognized by the individual with respect to the exercise of their Non<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-Qualified</FONT> Stock Option, provided that such amount constitutes an ordinary and necessary business expense to the Company and is reasonable and the limitations of Code Sections&#x00a0;280G and 162(m)&#x00a0;do not apply.</P>
		<P CLASS="H3" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:italic;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="BoldItalic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:bold;">Restricted Shares</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">A participant will not be subject to tax upon the grant of an award of restricted shares unless the participant otherwise elects to be taxed at the time of grant pursuant to Section&#x00a0;83(b)&#x00a0;of the Code. On the date an award of restricted share becomes transferable or is no longer subject to a substantial risk of forfeiture, the participant will recognize ordinary compensation income equal to the difference between the fair market value of the shares on that date over the amount the participant paid for such shares, if any. Such income will be subject to income tax withholdings, and the participant will be required to pay to the Company the amount of any required withholding </P>
		</DIV>

		<P CLASS="LRH" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:0;orphans:1;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin: 10px 0px 10px 0pt; page-break-after: always; border-bottom: 1px solid; margin-top: 10.0pt; margin-right: 0in; margin-bottom: 10.0pt; margin-left: 0in; text-align: center; font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: TimesNewRomanPSStd-Regular; color: black;">20</P>



	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;text-indent:0pt;margin-top:8pt;">taxes in respect to such income. If the participant made an election under Section&#x00a0;83(b)&#x00a0;of the Code, the participant will recognize ordinary compensation income at the time of grant equal to the difference between the fair market value of the shares on the date of grant over the amount the participant paid for such shares, if any, and any subsequent appreciation in the value of the shares will be treated as a capital gain upon sale of the shares. Special rules apply to the receipt and disposition of restricted shares received by officers and directors who are subject to Section&#x00a0;16(b)&#x00a0;of the U.S.&#x00a0;Securities Exchange&#x00a0;Act&#x00a0;of&#x00a0;1934. The Company or the participant&#x2019;s employer, as applicable, will be able to deduct, at the same time as it is recognized by the participant, the amount of taxable compensation to the participant for U.S.&#x00a0;federal income tax purposes, but such deduction may be limited under Sections 280G and 162(m)&#x00a0;of the Code for compensation paid to certain executives designated in those Sections.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Proposed Resolution</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">It is proposed that at the Meeting the following resolution be adopted:</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-left:24pt;text-indent:0pt;margin-top:8pt;">&#x201c;RESOLVED, that the U.S.&#x00a0;Taxpayers Appendix to the Kamada Ltd. 2011 Israeli Share Award Plan, attached as <FONT CLASS="CharOverride-3" STYLE="text-decoration:underline;">Appendix B</FONT> to the Proxy Statement for the Meeting, be, and hereby is, approved and adopted.&#x201d;</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Approval Required</FONT></P>

Under the policy, such services require the specific pre<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-approval</FONT> of our Audit Committee followed by ratification of our full Board of Directors. Any proposed services exceeding the pre<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-approval</FONT> amounts for all services to be provided by our independent auditor require an additional specific pre<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-approval</FONT> by our Audit Committee and approval of our Board of Directors.</P>

	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">
		<P CLASS="H1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:1;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">REVIEW AND DISCUSSION OF CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL STATEMENTS</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">In addition to considering the foregoing agenda items at the Meeting, we will also present our audited consolidated financial statements for the year ended December&#x00a0;31, 2021. This Item will not involve a shareholder vote.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Our audited consolidated financial statements for the year ended December&#x00a0;31, 2021, which form part of our annual report on Form&#x00a0;20<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-F</FONT> for the year ended December&#x00a0;31, 2021, filed with the SEC on March&#x00a0;15, 2022, are available under the &#x201c;Investor&#x00a0;&amp; Media&#x201d; portion of our website at <FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">www.kamada.com</FONT> or via the SEC&#x2019;s website at <FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">www.sec.gov</FONT> or the website of the ISA at <FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">www.magna.isa.gov.il</FONT>. Shareholders may receive a hard copy of the annual report on Form&#x00a0;20<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-F</FONT> containing the consolidated financial statements free of charge upon request. None of the audited consolidated financial statements, the Form&#x00a0;20<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-F</FONT> nor the contents of our website form part of the proxy solicitation material.</P>
		<P CLASS="H1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:1;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">SHAREHOLDER PROPOSALS</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Any shareholder who intends to present a proposal at the Meeting must satisfy the requirements of the Israeli Companies Law and the regulations promulgated thereunder. Under Section&#x00a0;66(b)&#x00a0;of the Israeli Companies Law, one or more shareholders who severally or jointly hold at least 1% of or outstanding voting rights are entitled to request that our Board of Directors include a proposal at a future shareholder meeting, provided that such proposal is appropriate for consideration by shareholders at such meeting, by submitting such proposal within seven&#x00a0;days of publication of a company&#x2019;s notice with respect to its general meeting of shareholders. Accordingly, any such shareholder(s)&#x00a0;may request to include a proposal on the agenda of the Meeting by submitting their proposals in writing to Ms. Yifat Philip, our Vice President General Counsel and Corporate Secretary, at the following address: 2 Holzman Street, Weizmann Science Park, Rehovot, Israel. For a shareholder proposal to be considered for inclusion at the Meeting, our Vice President General Counsel and Corporate Secretary must receive the written proposal, together with the accompanying documentation and information required to be submitted under Israeli law, no later than November&#x00a0;15, 2022. If our Board of Directors determines that a shareholder proposal is duly and timely received and is appropriate under applicable Israeli law for inclusion on the agenda of the Meeting, we will publish a revised agenda for the Meeting no later than November&#x00a0;22, 2022, by way of issuing a press release or submitting a Report on Form&#x00a0;6<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-K</FONT> to the SEC.</P>
		<P CLASS="H1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:1;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">OTHER MATTERS</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Our Board of Directors does not intend to bring any matters before the Meeting other than those specifically set forth in the Notice of Annual General Meeting of Shareholders and knows of no matters to be brought before the Meeting by others. If any other matters properly come before the Meeting, it is the intention of the persons named in the accompanying proxy to vote such proxy in accordance with the judgment and recommendation of the Board of Directors.</P>
		<P CLASS="H1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:1;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">ADDITIONAL INFORMATION</FONT></P>

The Company fulfills these obligations by filing reports with the SEC.&#x00a0;Our filings with the SEC are available to the public on the SEC&#x2019;s website at <FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">www.sec.gov</FONT>, the ISA&#x2019;s website at <FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">www.magna.isa.gov.il</FONT>, the TASE&#x2019;s website at <FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">http:</FONT><FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">//</FONT><FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">maya.tase.co.il</FONT> and under the &#x201c;Investor&#x00a0;&amp; Media&#x201d; portion of our website at <FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">www.kamada.com</FONT>. The contents of our website do not form part of the proxy solicitation material.</P>

					<TD CLASS="TB" STYLE="border-bottom-width:0pt;border-left-width:0pt;border-right-width:0pt;border-top-width:0pt;padding-bottom:3pt;padding-left:3pt;padding-right:3pt;padding-top:3pt;vertical-align:bottom;padding-bottom:2pt;padding-left:0pt;padding-right:0pt;padding-top:2pt;vertical-align:top;width: 94.44%; padding: 0in 0in 3px 0in;border-width: 0pt;" VALIGN="top">
						<P CLASS="Texttable" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:0;orphans:1;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;"><FONT CLASS="CharOverride-6" STYLE="font-size:9pt;font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;">Please sign exactly as your name or names appear on this Proxy. When shares are held jointly, each holder should sign. When signing as executor, administrator, attorney, trustee or guardian, please give full title as such. If the signer is a corporation, please sign full corporate name by duly authorized officer, giving full title as such. If signer is a partnership, please sign in partnership name by authorized person.</FONT></P>
					</TD>
				</TR>

		</TABLE>
